# Skills · G · part 35

861 entries, genobi-camera to genre-energy-guidance. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **genobi-camera**: Generate a Genobi shot with the right model - one workflow card per model the studio can run (LTX-2.5 Fast, LTX-2.5 Acoustic Space, LTX-2.5 Pro, FLUX 3 video, FLUX Video Upscale, FLUX 3 Image Klein, FLUX 3 Image and Edit, H3 and MiniMax Music 3), each with its route, lengths, sizes, cost, prompt guidance and the camera wording it accepts, always behind a quote the person accepts. ([koshimazaki/genobi](https://github.com/koshimazaki/genobi/tree/HEAD/plugins/genobi/skills/genobi-camera) · ★25)
- **genobi-cli**: Use the Genobi studio from a terminal when MCP is unavailable or a shell workflow is preferred. Discover the same tools and schemas, reuse named connection/model profiles, work with shared project Taste memory, and run local Dual Swords media operations. Does not install an agent or approve generation. ([koshimazaki/genobi](https://github.com/koshimazaki/genobi/tree/HEAD/plugins/genobi/skills/genobi-cli) · ★25)
- **genobi-comfy**: Extend Genobi through ComfyUI as its Developer - add a workflow graph and register it as a template, check it against the run location's node and model catalogue, run it on a local ComfyUI, Comfy Cloud or a Comfy Developer Platform deployment, add or fix a custom node, and rebuild the deployment when a graph needs nodes Cloud does not hold. Every paid run is quoted and waits for the person's yes; remote writes and deploys stay within the person's scope. ([koshimazaki/genobi](https://github.com/koshimazaki/genobi/tree/HEAD/plugins/genobi/skills/genobi-comfy) · ★25)
- **genobi-develop**: When the person asks for a change to the Genobi studio itself (its UI, a tool, a skill, a workflow, a node), start a Developer session with genobi_develop instead of telling them to open the repository - it works in a fresh worktree from origin/main, runs the checks, and leaves a diff or opens a pull request. Never spends. ([koshimazaki/genobi](https://github.com/koshimazaki/genobi/tree/HEAD/plugins/genobi/skills/genobi-develop) · ★25)

- **genome-browser-review**: Use when visually reviewing variants, SVs, repeats, or splicing in a genome browser, or saving screenshots and review decisions. - **genome-browser-track-retrieval**: Use when a SeqCode command (e.g. scorePhastCons, produceTSSplots) requires the chromInfo, refGene, or a conservation track for a specific assembly (e.g. mouse mm9) that must first be downloaded and uncompressed from UCSC. - **genome-build-version-matching**: Use when quantifying transcript abundances from RNA-seq FASTQ files that feed isoform-level analyses (e.g. CanIsoNet), and transcript identifiers must match a specific Ensembl genome build such as human GRCh37.p13. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genome-build-version-matching))
- **genome-catalog-metrics-extraction**: Use when a metagenomic binning study reports genome yields (e.g. complete vs draft counts) and you need to reproduce or extract the per-category, per-phylum breakdown from abstract text, tables, or supplementary tables. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/genome-catalog-metrics-extraction))
- **genome-chromosome-coordinate-segmentation**: Use when multiple ChIP-seq samples with spike-in chromatin must be compared genome-wide and a common fixed-size bin grid (e.g. 1000-bp bins of hg19 and FLY-suffixed dm3 from ChromInfo.txt) is needed before per-bin counting and normalization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genome-chromosome-coordinate-segmentation))
- **genome-class-to-pathway-cooccurrence-counting**: Use when you have a set of MIBiG BGCs with SMILES and (sub)classes and need genome-class-to-NPClassifier-pathway match frequencies for scoring BGC-metabolite links. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/genome-class-to-pathway-cooccurrence-counting))
- **genome-cluster-count-validation**: Use when a paper reports a species-level genome bin count (e.g. 786 SGBs from 19,542 prokaryotic genomes at 95% ANI) and you must reproduce the clustering from the deposited genome FASTAs to verify it. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/genome-cluster-count-validation))
- **genome-cluster-family-scoring-computation**: Use when you have pre-processed AntiSMASH BGC annotations (optionally clustered via BigScape into GCFs), GNPS molecular networking spectra and molecular families, and you seek to computationally link biosynthetic gene clusters to observed metabolites without manual curation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/genome-cluster-family-scoring-computation) · 2 copies)
- **genome-collection-index-construction**: Use when a collection of FASTA/MultiLine FASTA/FASTQ datasets (gzipped or not) must be indexed so that future sequence queries can list which datasets contain the query, with bounded RAM, disk footprint, and construction time. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genome-collection-index-construction))

- **genome-completeness-categorization-checkm-prokka**: Use when final cleaned scaffold FASTAs from hybrid metagenome assemblies (e.g. MetaPlatanus, metaFlye, MEGAHIT, OPERA-MS on 18 human gut samples with &gt;1 Gbp ONT plus Illumina paired-end reads) must be categorized as over-half or near-complete bacterial genomes for assembler benchmarking. - **genome-completeness-contamination-assessment**: Use when candidate bins have been produced from long-read contig embeddings (e.g., per-epsilon DBSCAN outputs in SemiBin2) and each bin must be scored for completeness, contamination, and F1 before greedy ensemble selection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/genome-completeness-contamination-assessment))
- **genome-completeness-contamination-estimation**: Use when you have a set of metagenome-assembled genomes (FASTA files) and need per-genome completeness/contamination estimates plus a defensible quality-tier classification (e.g. medium/high/near-complete) before downstream analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/genome-completeness-contamination-estimation))
- **genome-completeness-marker-filtering**: Use when a COG presence/absence feature matrix (e.g. 97 genomes x 30,455 COGs) is being prepared for trait-prediction training and each genome must pass a marker-COG completeness screen before model fitting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/genome-completeness-marker-filtering))
- **genome-completeness-prior-simulation**: Use when you need to benchmark or stress-test a core-genome estimator (e.g. mOTUpan) against simulated MAG/SAG incompleteness, or quantify how low-completeness genomes bias core-gene calls and bootstrap false-positive rates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/genome-completeness-prior-simulation))
- **genome-completeness-rarefaction**: Use when you need to validate a core-genome prediction made on genomes of heterogeneous completeness (e.g. MAGs with CheckM completeness priors) by simulating synthetic genomes degraded to match the incompleteness of the real input set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/genome-completeness-rarefaction))
- **genome-completeness-simulation**: Use when metagenomic bins or otherwise incomplete genomes must be scored with trait classifiers, and you need to quantify how much balanced accuracy degrades as genome completeness drops before trusting predictions on partial input. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/genome-completeness-simulation))
- **genome-coordinate-annotation-via-ensembl-api**: Use when variant records carry gene assignments but lack genomic coordinates, and you must annotate each gene/variant with chromosome, start, and end positions to build Circos histogram/scatter/highlight inputs. - **genome-coordinate-based-gene-locus-retrieval**: Use when you have a genome-guided annotated GTF (e.g. annotate_my_genomes final_annotated.gtf) and need to isolate all transcripts of one gene locus for isoform or protein-level verification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/genome-coordinate-based-gene-locus-retrieval))
- **genome-coordinate-liftover-harmonization**: Use when annotation reference databases or BED/GFF resources are provided in different genome builds (e.g. hg18, hg19, hg38) than the genome used for read alignment, and coordinates must be converted before overlap-based annotation. - **genome-coordinate-windowing-and-extraction**: Use when a genome annotation (GTF) and reference genome (e.g., hg19 FASTA) must be turned into fixed-length, strand-corrected sequence fragments centered on candidate residues for downstream model prediction. - **genome-cover-recurrence-modeling**: Use when sam alignments against the WoL reference database contain low-coverage hits that may be reference-overlap artifacts, and you need a model-predicted versus observed genome cover comparison to judge whether hits are true contaminants or spurious. - **genome-cover-thresholding**: Use when short shotgun reads spuriously map to short common motifs or transposable elements against a large reference (e.g., Web of Life), creating ambiguous low-coverage false positives that per-sample coverage cannot resolve because shotgun sequencing is shallow. - **genome-coverage-estimation-from-seed-sets**: Use when selecting or validating sparse short seed fragments (e.g. 270 bp or 285 bp FASTA seeds) for a long-read scaffolding pipeline and you need to report or target a specific genome coverage. - **genome-coverage-profile-computation-from-assigned-reads**: Use when metagenomic reads (e.g., paired-end FASTQ such as KLyo_C) have been classified against a reference database and you need per-genome window coverage profiles to identify false-positive candidate genomes before final taxonomic assignment. - **genome-report**: Generate prescriber-facing reports and import biomarker lab results. Triggers on: /biomarker, /wallet-card, "import lab results", "analyze bloodwork", "generate prescriber summary", "create wallet card", "update PGx card". ([glebis/genome-toolkit](https://github.com/glebis/genome-toolkit/tree/HEAD/skills/genome-report) · ★43)
- **genome-retrieval**: Recipes for downloading reference genomes inside the BioMysteryBench container. Use when a problem requires a full reference assembly (e.g. hg38, mm10, GRCh38). Two strategies: (A) background wget for large assemblies that take &gt;5 min to download (avoids the per-command timeout); (B) per-chromosome UCSC fetch for small regions spanning ≤5 chromosomes. ([iandonaldson/bio-mystery-bench](https://github.com/iandonaldson/bio-mystery-bench/tree/HEAD/SKILLS/genome-retrieval) · ★2)
- **genome-scaffolding-with-reference-alignment**: Use when existing contig assemblies (e.g., hifiasm output from ultra-low-coverage HG002/NA19240 HiFi reads) must be placed onto chromosomes against CHM13 (v2.0) and inter-chromosomal mis-scaffolding must be counted and compared between assemblers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/genome-scaffolding-with-reference-alignment))
- **genome-scale-bgc-tool-benchmarking**: Use when comparing BGC prediction tool performance (counts and prediction-set overlap) across a large genome collection, e.g. reproducing DeepBGC 10926 vs antiSMASH 13865 vs ClusterFinder 62491 on 3376 RefSeq genomes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/genome-scale-bgc-tool-benchmarking))
- **genome-scale-community-model-construction**: Use when the input is a set of genome-scale single-organism SBML metabolic models (e.g. AGORA members) and the goal is a reusable, member-attributable community metabolic model for FBA, exchange-metabolite, or cross-feeding analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/genome-scale-community-model-construction))
- **genome-scale-metabolic-flux-modeling-workflow**: Use when you have a genome-scale constraint-based metabolic model (GEM, SBML/JSON) for one or more organisms and want predicted flux states grounded in your own omics data — integrate transcriptomics / metabolomics-derived constraints (eFlux-style Reaction Activity/Propensity Scores, extracellular uptake-secretion rates) into the model, sample the feasible flux space with optGpSampler, interpret and compare the resulting flux distributions across samples or conditions, and, when multiple organism or community-member models exist, gap-fill and merge them into a consensus community model (COMMI… ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/workflows/genome-scale-metabolic-flux-modeling))
- **genome-scale-metabolic-model-constraint-editing**: Use when a human GEM (e.g. RECON3D in SBML/XML) must be updated with flux information from gut microbiota models — i.e. host reactions containing metabolites present in the microbiota model need their lower bounds rewritten. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/genome-scale-metabolic-model-constraint-editing))
- **genome-scale-metabolic-model-curation**: Use when a workflow requires a simulation-ready GEM (e.g., for FVA-based shadow-price extraction) and only a raw published model file such as iJO1366.mat or Yeast-GEM.mat is available. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/genome-scale-metabolic-model-curation))
- **genome-scale-metabolic-model-data-handling**: Use when a pipeline needs curated GEM files (e.g., .mat models) converted to irreversible, optimized versions as the basis for computing reaction-level shadow prices used as MPI-prediction features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/genome-scale-metabolic-model-data-handling))
- **genome-scale-metabolic-model-media-perturbation**: Use when a constraint-based model pipeline (e.g., SARTRE shadow-price features) must be shown to be insensitive to medium composition — alternate carbon sources or limiting carbon/nitrogen/phosphorus uptake rates — relative to a glucose base model. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/genome-scale-metabolic-model-media-perturbation))
- **genome-scale-metabolic-model-quality-assessment**: Use when a newly constructed or revised genome-scale metabolic model (SBML, e.g. from BioModels) must be checked against community standards such as MEMOTE before publication or reuse in downstream analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/genome-scale-metabolic-model-quality-assessment))
- **genome-scale-metabolic-model-reconstruction**: Use when draft GSMMs built from metagenome-assembled genomes underpredict metabolic phenotypes (e.g. missing exchange reactions) and you want to recover missing KOs with HMM searches and evidence-based gapfilling before flux analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/genome-scale-metabolic-model-reconstruction))
- **genome-scale-metabolic-model-shadow-price-analysis**: Use when predicting metabolite-protein interactions from constraint-based models and you need physiologically grounded metabolite features (shadow prices) instead of, or alongside, chemical fingerprints. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/genome-scale-metabolic-model-shadow-price-analysis))
- **genome-scale-metabolic-model-simulation**: Use when a human GEM (RECON3D or similar, SBML/cobrapy-loadable) must be simulated with the metabolite pool adjusted to gut microbiota fluxes, or when the α-weighted trade-off between host bounds and microbial in-/out-fluxes must be swept. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/genome-scale-metabolic-model-simulation--46eda3c39ef42183))
- **genome-scale-metabolic-model-simulation**: Use when an SBML-encoded genome-scale metabolic model (e.g. BioModels MODEL2007210001, iHsaEC21, iAB-AMØ-1410) must be loaded, FBA-simulated for host/virus biomass objectives, or FVA-refined so reaction reversibility and flux ranges are determined. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/genome-scale-metabolic-model-simulation--7517058b062a813c))
- **genome-scale-metabolic-modeling**: Inspect and analyze constraint-based metabolic models with SBML/FBC, COBRApy, FBA, pFBA and FVA under explicit objectives and media; use for model-based steady-state predictions, not measured flux claims or proven therapeutic interventions. ([AbhignanVajjala/Bioinformatics-agent-skills](https://github.com/AbhignanVajjala/Bioinformatics-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/genome-scale-metabolic-modeling))
- **genome-scale-metabolic-modeling**: Use when transcriptomic data (bulk RNA-seq matrix, Seurat object, or cell-cluster expression profiles) must be converted into reaction-level flux distributions under a defined nutrient medium, e.g. nutrient-aware cancer metabolism analysis of TCGA or single-cell TME data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genome-scale-metabolic-modeling))
- **genome-scale-metabolic-network-reconstruction**: Use when starting from isolate genome assemblies/annotations (e.g., 432 At-SPHERE Arabidopsis root isolate genomes) and you need draft genome-scale metabolic models (SBML) from automated pipelines, or multiple draft reconstructions that must be harmonized, gap-filled, or merged into a consensus. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/genome-scale-metabolic-network-reconstruction--bf1bed4d49abf4d5))
- **genome-scale-metabolic-network-reconstruction**: Use when annotated protein FASTA, BLASTp/DIAMOND output, or a draft SBML model is available for a bacterial genome and a COBRApy-compatible genome-scale metabolic model (SBML GENRE) is needed for FBA, gene essentiality, or flux sampling analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/genome-scale-metabolic-network-reconstruction--ac27ffd91888f614))
- **genome-scale-metabolic-reaction-mapping**: Use when top ML predictor features (e.g. genes from CNV, methylation, transcriptomics, proteomics models) must be anchored to specific reactions in a genome-scale metabolic network like Recon3D to test whether predictors sit near the metabolite they predict. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/genome-scale-metabolic-reaction-mapping))
- **genome-scale-model-curation-and-irreversibility-conversion**: Use when a constraint-based modeling pipeline needs reaction-level dual values (shadow prices) and requires the model to be converted to an irreversible form with biomass constrained to 90% of its optimum before optimization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/genome-scale-model-curation-and-irreversibility-conversion))
- **genome-scale-model-validation-and-curation**: Use when you have multiple draft genome-scale metabolic reconstructions in standard formats (JSON, XML, SBML) representing individual community members or assembly variants, and you need to verify they are structurally sound and consistently annotated before merging them into a consensus model or. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/genome-scale-model-validation-and-curation))
- **genome-scale-sequence-screening**: Use when you must detect and type Dsr-dependent dissimilatory sulphur metabolism proteins across thousands of complete genomes or (meta)genomic protein records on a personal computer, without reconstructing phylogenies or running blast-based marker searches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/genome-scale-sequence-screening))
- **genome-self-alignment-analysis**: Use when comparing methylation states between duplicated/paralogous genomic regions: you have a genome reference FASTA and need paired homologous regions to test differential cytosine methylation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/genome-self-alignment-analysis))
- **genome-sequence-extraction**: Use when a user needs to download the complete configured genome FASTA and GFF3 for a Potato Agent Genome Browser assembly, or deterministically extract strand-aware regions, genes, promoters, transcripts, CDS, or proteins from potato and other plant assemblies. Supports readable local resources and the public Interface API without requiring a Potato Agent account. ([biojiayuxin/potato-agent](https://github.com/biojiayuxin/potato-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/potato-knowledge-bioinformatics/genome-sequence-extraction) · ★1)
- **genome-sequence-matching-for-nucleotide-patterns**: Use when you have filtered peak or chromatin accessibility counts and need to annotate each peak with the presence or absence of specific DNA sequence patterns—either predefined motifs (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/genome-sequence-matching-for-nucleotide-patterns))
- **genome-sequence-mining**: Use when you have assembled genomic DNA sequences (contigs in FASTA format, not antiSMASH or BOA output) and corresponding LC-MS/MS data (in MGF, mzXML, mzML, or mzData format) from the same organism, and you want to identify novel RiPPs by linking gene cluster predictions to observed mass spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/genome-sequence-mining) · 3 copies)
- **genome-sequence-reconstruction-with-variant-masking**: Use when performing allele-specific expression (ASE) analysis in an F1 hybrid cross (e.g., CAST×B6) and reads must be assigned to parental origin via a strain-specific or diploid reference rather than a single generic reference genome. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genome-sequence-reconstruction-with-variant-masking))
- **genome-set-retrieval-from-ncbi**: Use when building the raw-genome foundation for a pan-biosynthetic analysis: you need complete (or WGS draft) genomes for a taxon from NCBI, with outgroup genomes, before BGC prediction and GCF clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/genome-set-retrieval-from-ncbi))
- **genome-shuffling-breakpoint-simulation**: Use when you need a controlled ground-truth benchmark of structural-variant callers on long-read data, with recall/FPR measured per caller across a range of downsampled coverage levels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/genome-shuffling-breakpoint-simulation))

([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/genome-signature-characterization))
- **genome-signature-plasmid-prediction**: Use when metagenomic or genome assembly contigs (ideally &gt;1000 bp, FASTA) must be labelled plasmid vs chromosome without prior knowledge of sample taxonomic composition, e.g. plasmidome profiling of environmental assemblies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/genome-signature-plasmid-prediction))
- **genome-size-based-preset-selection**: Use when mapping raw nanopore signals (FAST5/POD5/SLOW5/BLOW5) to a reference genome with RawHash2/RawHash and you must choose the preset matching the reference genome's size and type (viral vs. human-sized). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/genome-size-based-preset-selection))
- **genome-size-heterozygosity-estimation**: Use when starting a de novo assembly of an uncharacterized eukaryote genome and you need an estimate of haploid genome size, repeat content, and heterozygosity from raw short reads before choosing assembly parameters. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/genome-size-heterozygosity-estimation))
- **genome-size-metric-recording**: Use when reproducing or reporting genome yield tables (complete vs draft bin counts) and per-phylum distributions of curated genomes from metagenomic binning results. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/genome-size-metric-recording))
- **genome-skimming-marker-retrieval**: Use when you need to mine single-copy nuclear markers (e.g., OrthoDB USCOs) for phylogenomics from genome-skimming (LC-WGS) data provided as Illumina short reads or de novo contigs, especially at coverages of 1x–40x. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/genome-skimming-marker-retrieval))
- **genome-softmask**: Use when soft-masking a genome assembly with de novo repeat discovery and RepeatMasker. Provides a reusable Apptainer + Dfam TETools workflow for running RepeatModeler first, then RepeatMasker softmasking per genome or haplotype. ([biojiayuxin/potato-agent](https://github.com/biojiayuxin/potato-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/potato-knowledge-bioinformatics/genome-softmask) · ★1)
- **genome-space-modification-aggregation**: Use when NanoSpeech basecalled fasta/q read headers carry modification (inosine or multi-mod) indexes and you need them mapped to reference coordinates and aggregated into per-site modification counts/frequencies. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genome-space-modification-aggregation))
- **genome-structure-class-pairing-frequency-analysis**: Use when you need to bridge genome-mining BGC class ontologies (antiSMASH/MIBiG) with structure-based chemical ontologies (NPClassifier/ClassyFire) to derive empirical class-matching score tables for filtering BGC–MS/MS links. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/genome-structure-class-pairing-frequency-analysis))
- **genome-synteny**: 使用 Snakemake 控制基因组共线性分析；基于 Python MCScan/jcvi 和最长 CDS 代表转录本注释/序列，支持 macrosynteny 和指定基因区间 microsynteny，默认输入蛋白序列、--cscore=0.9，并输出 PDF 矢量图。 ([biojiayuxin/potato-agent](https://github.com/biojiayuxin/potato-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/potato-knowledge-bioinformatics/genome-synteny) · ★1)
- **genome-tiling-chunk-overhang-configuration**: Use when preparing a ChIP-Seq count dataset for genome-wide GAM fitting with GenoGAM, i.e. when BAM alignments must be tiled into overlapping intervals of fixed chunkSize/overhangSize and written to an HDF5 backend. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genome-tiling-chunk-overhang-configuration))
- **genome-tracks**: --&gt; ([mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia](https://github.com/mdbabumiamssm/AI-Agentic-Skills-by-Dr.-Mia/tree/HEAD/Skills/Data_Visualization/data-visualization/genome-tracks) · ★7)
- **genome-transcriptome-coordinate-transform**: Use when phasiRNA/siRNA clusters were predicted separately against a reference genome and a reference transcriptome and need to be unified into a single, deduplicated PHAS locus set with gene annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genome-transcriptome-coordinate-transform))
- **genome-validate**: Multi-agent validation pipeline for fact-checking claims, verifying evidence tiers, and ensuring prescriber document accuracy. Uses Codex CLI, NotebookLM, PubMed subagents, and Tavily search. Supports: audit (full vault), gene SYMBOL (single gene fact-check), protocol NAME (protocol/report fact-check). Triggers on: /genome-validate, "validate this note", "fact-check", "cross-check claims", "verify evidence", "audit this report", "fact-check gene X", "check protocol X". ([glebis/genome-toolkit](https://github.com/glebis/genome-toolkit/tree/HEAD/skills/genome-validate) · ★43)
- **genome-variant-analysis**: Build reproducible workflows for VCF, BCF, population variants, annotation, and genotype-derived analyses. Use when work involves genome builds, variant normalization, allele harmonization, cohort QC, annotation databases, or genomic interval operations. ([anonymous-temp/EviMed-Science](https://github.com/anonymous-temp/EviMed-Science/tree/HEAD/OpenScience/runtime/skills/curated-scientific/genome-variant-analysis) · ★1)
- **genome-wide-alpha-distribution-comparison**: Use when you need to test whether a biologically defined subset of protein-coding genes (e.g. by recombination rate bin or chromosome localization) shows a different alpha than the genome-wide gene set, using DAF and divergence tables. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/genome-wide-alpha-distribution-comparison))
- **genome-wide-association-study-analysis**: Use when testing whether ukbnmr's removal of technical variation from NMR biomarker data increases genetic association power, or when counting genome-wide significant loci (P &lt; 5e-8) for a metabolomics trait. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/genome-wide-association-study-analysis))
- **genome-wide-correlation-analysis**: Use when you have CscoreTool-M *_cscore.txt outputs and a reference set of compartment/sub-compartment scores for the same 500kb hg38 windows and need a genome-wide Pearson correlation to judge inference accuracy or depth robustness. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genome-wide-correlation-analysis))
- **genome-wide-coverage-quantification**: Use when nanopore (or other long-read) alignment (BAM from minimap2 vs GRCh38) must be converted into per-sample, genome-wide coverage to genotype SV/TE insertion and deletion genotypes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/genome-wide-coverage-quantification--53e1272d333585fd))
- **genome-wide-coverage-quantification**: Use when characterizing a nanopore whole-genome dataset (e.g., FASTQ read lengths plus per-aligner BAM coverage) before or alongside structural-variant calling, to benchmark sequencing depth and alignment quality. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/genome-wide-coverage-quantification--a8a05d85f399c0d1))
- **genome-wide-dh-prediction-evaluation**: Use when you have BIRD-predicted DH matrices (log2(x+1)) plus true DNase-seq profiles for multiple cell types and need to quantify prediction accuracy overall, for differential DH, or versus ChromImpute. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genome-wide-dh-prediction-evaluation))
- **genome-wide-differential-region-validation**: Use when reproducing or validating published genome-wide differential ChIP-seq region calls for broad histone marks (e.g., H3K9me3/H3K27me3) with histoneHMM and a quantitative target such as total differential Mb or genome fraction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genome-wide-differential-region-validation))
- **genome-wide-gam-smoothing-fit**: Use when you need base-level smoothing and significance/peak calls for ChIP-Seq protein-DNA occupancy across whole chromosomes or gigabase-scale genomes (e.g., TF binding, genotype contrasts) without ad-hoc binning or windowing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genome-wide-gam-smoothing-fit))
- **genome-wide-occupancy-profile-scoring**: Use when comparing TF binding site predictions from chromatin occupancy models (RoboCOP, COMPETE, MACS2+FIMO/FIMO baselines) against ChIP-chip, ChIP-exo, or ORGANIC annotations to quantify and rank method accuracy. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genome-wide-occupancy-profile-scoring))
- **genome-wide-orf-counting-acquisition-modes**: Use when benchmarking genome-mining candidate generation on a genome set (e.g., 36 ACTI RefSeq FASTA assemblies) where you need per-genome BGC and RiPP counts from antiSMASH plus short-ORF candidate counts under both motif-ORF and all-ORF modes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/genome-wide-orf-counting-acquisition-modes))
- **genome-wide-phosphoproteome-scoring**: Use when you have a complete proteome FASTA (e.g. wheat, oat, barley, maize) and pre-trained PhosBoost models, and need phosphorylation probability scores for all S/T/Y residues plus browser visualization tracks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/genome-wide-phosphoproteome-scoring))
- **genome-wide-proteomics-frailty**: Tier A personal readout, checked against independent implementations: the paper prints no worked example, so no printed result was reproduced. Writes a personal Hospital Frailty Risk Score by summing published ICD-10 condition weights, then places that sum in a low, intermediate, or high bin. Use when the user brings diagnosis codes or an already computed score, medicines, or checkup labs and asks about this hospital frailty score. The skill does not ask for a reference cohort. It does not turn the bin into advice to start or stop a medicine. ([zwbao/longevity-skills](https://github.com/zwbao/longevity-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/genome-wide-proteomics-frailty) · ★1)
- **genome-wide-scaffold-aggregation**: Use when you have inStrain profile output per sample (computed against concatenated representative sub-species .fasta references) and need per-sub-species genome-level microdiversity metrics (e.g. nucleotide diversity of Klebsiella) for group-level comparison such as birth mode. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/genome-wide-scaffold-aggregation))
- **genome-wide-signal-binning**: Use when filtered .bam/.bai alignments from a ChIPseq/RNAseq pipeline must be converted into compressed genome-browser tracks (.wig/.bigwig) or compact GRanges objects for visualization and downstream analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genome-wide-signal-binning))
- **genome-wide-tfbs-prediction-scanning**: Use when an in vitro trained TF affinity model (e.g. DNAffinity on gcPBM data for CBF1) must be applied genome-wide to predict in vivo binding sites and validated against exo peak calls. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genome-wide-tfbs-prediction-scanning))
- **genome-wide-variant-calling-sms-reads**: Use when you need to call germline SNPs/small indels genome-wide from BAM alignments of Illumina (50x), PacBio NGMLR-aligned, or ONT rel3 R9.4 reads, and evaluate Precision/Recall/F1 against a GIAB truth set. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/genome-wide-variant-calling-sms-reads))
- **genome_annotation**: Genome Annotation Pipeline - Annotate a genome: NCBI annotation report, Ensembl gene lookup, UCSC tracks, and KEGG pathway links. Use this skill for genomics tasks involving get genome annotation report get lookup symbol list tracks kegg link. Combines 4 tools from 4 SCP server(s). ([SpectrAI-Initiative/InnoClaw](https://github.com/SpectrAI-Initiative/InnoClaw/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/genome_annotation) · ★399 · 4 copies)
- **genomeark-aws**: Access and navigate GenomeArk AWS S3 bucket - VGP assemblies, QC data, and species directory structure ([Delphine-L/claude_global](https://github.com/Delphine-L/claude_global/tree/HEAD/skills/vgp/genomeark-aws))
- **genomecanvas-differential**: GenomeCanvas exploratory differential figures - differential-peak volcano between two sample groups over BED regions, and peak-center comparison heatmaps between region sets. Use for a quick look; formal differential binding needs DESeq2/edgeR/DiffBind. ([icefoxx/genomeCanvas](https://github.com/icefoxx/genomeCanvas/tree/HEAD/skills/genomecanvas-differential))
- **genomecanvas-enrichment**: GenomeCanvas region enrichment figures in the style of deepTools computeMatrix/plotHeatmap/plotProfile - signal heatmaps, mean profiles, profile + heatmap and k-means/hierarchical clustered heatmaps around region centres, TSS/TES or scaled region bodies. ([icefoxx/genomeCanvas](https://github.com/icefoxx/genomeCanvas/tree/HEAD/skills/genomecanvas-enrichment))
- **genomecanvas-plot**: Render any GenomeCanvas Studio figure (genomic tracks, enrichment heatmaps/profiles, correlation/PCA/PairGrid, differential volcano/peak-center, signal transformations) from BigWig/bedGraph, BED and GTF files, either locally in-process or by submitting a job to a running Studio web server (agent mode). Start here; it routes to one skill per plot family. ([icefoxx/genomeCanvas](https://github.com/icefoxx/genomeCanvas/tree/HEAD/skills/genomecanvas-plot))
- **genomecanvas-relationships**: GenomeCanvas sample-relationship figures - correlation heatmap, PCA scatter (with interactive HTML) and PairGrid scatter matrix over BED regions, genome-wide bins or sliding windows (like deepTools multiBigwigSummary + plotCorrelation/plotPCA), plus Sample QC that flags mislabelled, swapped and low-quality samples in large collections from a sample sheet and a bins x samples matrix. ([icefoxx/genomeCanvas](https://github.com/icefoxx/genomeCanvas/tree/HEAD/skills/genomecanvas-relationships))
- **genomecanvas-tracks**: GenomeCanvas genome-browser style locus figures - stacked signal tracks, overlaid signals, and gene/transcript models with peaks, chromHMM states and highlight windows. Use when the user wants to show signal at one genomic locus. ([icefoxx/genomeCanvas](https://github.com/icefoxx/genomeCanvas/tree/HEAD/skills/genomecanvas-tracks))
- **genomecanvas-transform**: GenomeCanvas signal transformations at one locus - difference, ratio, log2 ratio between two signals, and local background correction - drawn as A, B and the derived track. Use to compare two tracks (e.g. treatment vs control, ChIP vs input) in a region. ([icefoxx/genomeCanvas](https://github.com/icefoxx/genomeCanvas/tree/HEAD/skills/genomecanvas-transform))
- **genomescope-model-fitting**: Use when raw Illumina FASTQ reads are available and an assembly-independent estimate of haploid genome size, repeat content, and heterozygosity is needed to validate or contextualize a draft genome assembly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/genomescope-model-fitting))
- **genomespot-predictions**: Look up GenomeSPOT-predicted growth conditions — optimal / minimum / maximum temperature, pH, salinity, and oxygen tolerance — for a sequenced genome by its NCBI assembly accession (`GCA_*` / `GCF_*`). Covers tens of thousands of bacterial and archaeal genomes. Use whenever the user asks about predicted growth optima for an organism by NCBI accession or species, wants to identify thermophiles / psychrophiles / halophiles / alkaliphiles / anaerobes by prediction, or needs growth-condition predictions for a genome that is not a CVM strain. ([cultivarium/hermes-starter-kit](https://github.com/cultivarium/hermes-starter-kit/tree/HEAD/skills/genomespot-predictions) · ★1)
- **genometrics**: Run local quality control on FASTQ files (single-end or R1/R2 pairs) and summarize VCF files, then compare files or explain a QC check. Use when the user asks to QC sequencing reads, check a FASTQ or VCF, summarize variants, or compare samples. Runs entirely on this machine with Python 3.10+ and no dependencies. ([BrunoFOmena/Genometrics-Project](https://github.com/BrunoFOmena/Genometrics-Project/tree/HEAD/skills/genometrics))
- **genomex**: Run and interpret the GenomeX genome-QC pipeline on bacterial (or fungal) assemblies - assembly stats, gene calling, BUSCO-style completeness, contamination detection, ANI, and core/accessory gene-content comparison between isolates. Use when asked to QC a genome, check completeness or contamination, compute ANI, compare two genomes' gene content, or ask why two isolates differ. ([Hasnat-GPU/GenomeX](https://github.com/Hasnat-GPU/GenomeX/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/genomex) · ★1)
- **genomi**: Use this skill for genetics, genome source, variant, gene, phenotype, disease, screen, pharmacogenomics, and Genomi install/setup maintenance questions. ([exon-research/genomi](https://github.com/exon-research/genomi/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★493 · 2 copies)
- **genomi**: ../../../SKILL.md ([exon-research/genomi](https://github.com/exon-research/genomi/tree/HEAD/skills/host-agent/genomi) · ★493 · 69 copies)
- **genomi-decode**: Activate this skill for "/genomi decode", "decode my genome", "decode my DNA", "show me the dashboard", "the Genomi dashboard", "one-shot rundown", or any all-at-once request that asks Genomi to compose every capability's findings into a single artifact. This is the whole-genome dashboard kicker — it sweeps every relevant Genomi capability in one shot, not a per-target lookup. Composes evidence from every relevant Genomi capability into a single self-contained Genomi Dashboard.html and returns localhost serve metadata. Active genome required. ([exon-research/genomi](https://github.com/exon-research/genomi/tree/HEAD/skills/decode) · ★493)
- **genomi-decode**: Activate this skill for "/genomi decode", "decode my genome", "decode my DNA", "show me the dashboard", "the Genomi dashboard", "full report", "one-shot rundown", or any all-at-once request that asks Genomi to compose every capability's findings into a single artifact. This is the whole-genome dashboard kicker — it sweeps every relevant Genomi capability in one shot, not a per-target lookup. Composes evidence from every relevant Genomi capability into a single self-contained Genomi Dashboard.html and returns localhost serve metadata. Active genome required. ([yo-steven/genomi-exploration-20260613](https://github.com/yo-steven/genomi-exploration-20260613/tree/HEAD/skills/decode))
- **genomi-gnomad**: Fetch reusable public population allele frequencies from gnomAD for a specific variant. Use when the user asks about allele frequency, MAF, population stratification, gnomAD numbers, or rarity of a specific allele. ([exon-research/genomi](https://github.com/exon-research/genomi/tree/HEAD/skills/gnomad) · ★493 · 2 copies)
- **genomic-alteration-significance-testing**: Use when you have per-sample attention-head features from a pretrained tGPT model over tumor bulk transcriptomes and need to link them to mutation, HRD, genome-doubling, TMB, or survival annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genomic-alteration-significance-testing))
- **genomic-analysis**: Apply Genomic Analysis in computational biology and life sciences. Use when analyzing biological data, developing therapeutics, or building bioinformatics pipelines. This skill covers methodologies, tools, and applications in genomic analysis. ([Sandeeprdy1729/skill_galaxy](https://github.com/Sandeeprdy1729/skill_galaxy/tree/HEAD/skills/bio/genomic-analysis) · ★1)
- **genomic-annotation-command-line-workflow-execution**: Use when you have a GFF/GTF gene model plus a BAM of aligned reads (e.g. 10x Chromium scRNA-seq) and need to annotate or refine 3' UTR features at the genome level. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genomic-annotation-command-line-workflow-execution))
- **genomic-annotation-cross-referencing**: Use when a set of variants or genes identified by identifiers (GENCODE v19 gene symbols and/or Ensembl gene IDs) must be joined to per-gene quantitative scores (MRSD read depths) across biosamples to yield a per-variant classification and an aggregate fraction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genomic-annotation-cross-referencing))
- **genomic-annotation-feature-engineering**: Use when building or retraining a variant pathogenicity predictor that needs annotated per-variant features (allele frequency, conservation, deleteriousness scores) extracted from VCF-level variant calls. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/genomic-annotation-feature-engineering))
- **genomic-bed-data-loading**: Use when reproducing a DNALONGBENCH long-range task (eQTL, RSAP, TISP, contact map, enhancer-target gene) and raw bed-file data downloaded from a Dataverse DOI must be converted into train/valid/test loaders for model training and inference. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-bed-data-loading))
- **genomic-bed-file-strand-annotation**: Use when strand-oriented genomic features (e.g. GENCODE v33 genes, PolyASite PAS clusters, UCSC tfbsConsSites TFBSs, JASPAR/FIMO motif tracks) must be converted into 4-column BED files before running Asymmetron asymmetry analyses. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-bed-file-strand-annotation))
- **genomic-bgc-extraction-antismash**: Use when when you have genomic DNA sequences (from isolates or metagenomes) and need to identify and characterize biosynthetic gene clusters as input for downstream natural product linkage analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/genomic-bgc-extraction-antismash))
- **genomic-bias-correction-with-local-features**: Use when raw N*N single-cell Hi-C contact matrices are sparse and biased by local genomic features (effective length, GC content, fragment-end mappability) and must be normalized before DCI testing with scHiCDiff.KS/CVM/NB/ZINB. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genomic-bias-correction-with-local-features))
- **genomic-bin-assignment-from-chromosome-sizes**: Use when a PET/interaction bedpe file must be aggregated into a fixed-bin (e.g. 10 kb) 2D contact map whose bin grid is bounded by a chromosome sizes file such as chrom_hg19.sizes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-bin-assignment-from-chromosome-sizes))
- **genomic-bin-count-aggregation**: Use when raw single-cell ChIP-seq/ATAC-seq reads (FASTQ→BAM/BED) or per-cell alignment files must be converted into a cell-by-region count matrix binned across the genome for downstream clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-bin-count-aggregation))
- **genomic-bin-labeling**: Use when you already have per-bin node embeddings from a GAE trained on 400×400 Hi-C submatrices and need an unsupervised cluster label per genomic bin to delineate TAD boundaries. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-bin-labeling))
- **genomic-bin-read-counting**: Use when aligned ChIP-seq BAM files for experimental and spike-in genomes must be converted into genome-wide binned read-count tables (e.g. chromosome-start-end-count rows at 1-kbp resolution) before cross-condition normalization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-bin-read-counting))
- **genomic-bin-segmentation**: Use when preparing a spikChIP or similar spike-in ChIP-seq pipeline: before counting reads per bin or classifying bins as peaks/background, the sample and spike-in genomes must be divided into fixed-size bins from a ChromInfo chromosome list. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-bin-segmentation))
- **genomic-binned-variant-summarization**: Use when you have heterozygous SNP/small-indel genotype data in a VCF and need per-bin summaries (e.g., BAF likelihood profiles at 10 kb or 100 kb bin sizes) to support CNV/CNA or copy number-neutral LOH analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/genomic-binned-variant-summarization))
- **genomic-binning-of-modification-calls**: Use when per-tool m6A calls in BED format must be scored against a gold-standard peaks BED to compute TP/FP/FN and precision/recall/F1 in genomic bins, e.g. at default significance cut-offs or restricted bin sets (RRACH, high-coverage, exclusion-zone bins). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/genomic-binning-of-modification-calls))
- **genomic-binning-read-counting**: Use when preparing BAM/Bed ChIP-seq alignments of broad histone marks (e.g. H3K27me3, H3K9me3) as fixed-width binned read counts before HMM-based modification or differential-modification calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-binning-read-counting))
- **genomic-blacklist-filtering**: Use when building a DiffBind workflow on ATAC-seq or ChIP-seq peaks (e.g., mouse/mm10 cKO-vs-WT samples) before counting reads over consensus peaks, to exclude ENCODE blacklist artifact regions from downstream DAR generation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-blacklist-filtering))
- **genomic-blacklist-region-filtering**: Use when preparing narrowPeak/BED peak files (ChIP-seq, CUT&Tag, CUT&Run, ATAC-seq, DNase-seq) for cross-dataset comparison or QC, so that artifactual peaks in ENCODE blacklisted regions (hg19/hg38) do not bias overlap statistics, correlation plots, or ChromHMM/ChIPseeker annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-blacklist-region-filtering))
- **genomic-control-inflation-assessment-lambda-gc**: Use when benchmarking DE or gene set enrichment methods on scRNA-seq summary statistics and you need to check p-value calibration via a genomic control factor (lambda_gc), as in the iDEA paper's Fig. 4 comparison. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genomic-control-inflation-assessment-lambda-gc))
- **genomic-control-inflation-factor-estimation**: Use when benchmarking whether a competitive GSE method (iDEA, fGSEA, PAGE, GSEA, CAMERA) yields well-calibrated p-values under null simulations on zero-inflated scRNA-seq data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genomic-control-inflation-factor-estimation))
- **genomic-coordinate-alignment-of-prediction-tracks**: Use when two genome-wide DH or regulome track sets (e.g., BIRD predictions and ChromImpute imputations) must be compared across shared loci and cell types, requiring coordinate/locus matching before correlation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genomic-coordinate-alignment-of-prediction-tracks))
- **genomic-coordinate-binning**: Use when per-nucleotide scores (e.g. RSCanner structure counts) must be aggregated into fixed non-overlapping windows along a transcript to rank regions, e.g. locating high-structure-density segments in a long RNA. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genomic-coordinate-binning))
- **genomic-coordinate-clustering**: Use when you have an indexed BAM of long reads (ONT/PacBio) aligned with minimap2 or lra and need to call putative structural rearrangement breakpoints from split-read junction positions at a target locus. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/genomic-coordinate-clustering))
- **genomic-coordinate-conversion**: Use when you need to map computed per-bin metrics (insulation scores, boundary calls, contact frequencies) back to genomic coordinates for export to BED/GFF format, cross-reference with external annotations, or validate that computed features fall within expected genomic ranges. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/genomic-coordinate-conversion))
- **genomic-coordinate-distance-matching**: Use when validating a coordinate-level genome-wide prediction (e.g. called nucleosome dyads) against high-precision reference annotations, and you need a distance-thresholded precision-recall curve. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-coordinate-distance-matching))
- **genomic-coordinate-file-formatting**: Use when you have per-genomic-bin cluster labels (e.g., HDBSCAN output from TOAST embeddings) and must delineate contiguous same-label regions into a TAD boundary coordinate file at a stated matrix resolution. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-coordinate-file-formatting))
- **genomic-coordinate-format-unification**: Use when merging already annotated miRNAs from heterogeneous databases (miRBase, PmiREN, sRNAanno, PsRNA) with novel sRNA-Seq-derived precursor predictions that use inconsistent coordinate representations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genomic-coordinate-format-unification))
- **genomic-coordinate-integrity**: Checks genomic interval and variant coordinates across file formats and reference assemblies. Use when joining genomic datasets, converting intervals, reconciling contig names, or investigating potential off-by-one and reference mismatches. ([dandgabr/Coacus](https://github.com/dandgabr/Coacus/tree/HEAD/knowledge/skills/domains/academic/genomic-coordinate-integrity) · ★10)
- **genomic-coordinate-interval-analysis**: Use when a phased ONT BAM/VCF needs phase blocks joined within a specific coordinate window (e.g., HLA chr6:30,400,000-31,300,000) and SNV-only phasing left genes like HLA-E and HLA-C in separate blocks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/genomic-coordinate-interval-analysis))
- **genomic-coordinate-liftover**: Use when comparing SV call sets or truth sets built on different reference genome assemblies (e.g. a VCF on hg19 vs calls on GRCh38) and breakpoints must be mapped to one build before intersecting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/genomic-coordinate-liftover))
- **genomic-coordinate-mapping**: Use when after sub-clusters have been detected by PRESTO-STAT or PRESTO-TOP statistical methods on tokenised and redundancy-filtered BGCs, and you need to report their precise genomic positions, gene locus tags, and orientations for integration with natural product substructure predictions or for. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/genomic-coordinate-mapping))
- **genomic-coordinate-mapping**: Use when predicted phosphosites given in protein residue coordinates must be projected onto genome coordinates and merged with DIAMOND inferred positives into a GFF3 genome-browser track. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/genomic-coordinate-mapping))
- **genomic-coordinate-overlap-analysis**: Use when a single-sample germline VCF must be filtered to variants overlapping a gene panel BED (coding and noncoding regions), optionally restricted to diagnostic-grade (GREEN) PanelApp genes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/genomic-coordinate-overlap-analysis))
- **genomic-coordinate-overlap-comparison**: Use when comparing predicted UTR (or other interval) coordinates from multiple tools against a reference annotation and you need a tolerant agreement fraction rather than exact-match counting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genomic-coordinate-overlap-comparison))
- **genomic-coordinate-overlap-detection**: Use when you have CSV gene annotations (id, Chr, Strand, Start, End) for a genome and need to enumerate all antisense gene pairs whose loci overlap, stratified by overlap structure. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genomic-coordinate-overlap-detection))
- **genomic-coordinate-signature-tabulation**: Use when you have contig-to-reference alignments (BAM) from a diploid assembly (e.g., VolcanoSV-asm output on HiFi/CLR/ONT reads) and need to tabulate large INS/DEL SV signatures for downstream variant calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/genomic-coordinate-signature-tabulation))
- **genomic-coordinate-sorting**: Use when a fragment file produced by ExtractHair (modified from HapCUT2) must be sorted consistently with VCF variant order before SpecHap linkage-graph construction or haplotype phasing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-coordinate-sorting))
- **genomic-coordinate-translation-of-mirnas**: Use when annotated miRNA records from public plant databases and newly predicted precursors from sRNA-Seq must be merged into one non-redundant candidate set on a common reference genome. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genomic-coordinate-translation-of-mirnas))
- **genomic-coordinates**: Convert genomic intervals between coordinate conventions, normalise and compare variant representations, and detect assembly or contig-naming mismatches before they corrupt an analysis. - **genomic-coordinates**: Converts genomic intervals between coordinate conventions, normalises and compares variant representations, and detects assembly or contig-naming mismatches before they corrupt an analysis. Used whenever coordinates cross a format, tool, or assembly boundary - converting between BED, GFF/GTF, VCF, SAM/BAM, WIG, PSL, genePred, Picard interval_list, or region strings; reconciling 0-based half-open with 1-based inclusive; left-aligning or trimming indels; checking whether two variant records descri ([k-dense-ai/scientific-agent-skills](https://github.com/k-dense-ai/scientific-agent-skills) · 1.2k installs)
- **genomic-correlation-decay-visualization**: Use when integrating epigenomic p-values (e.g., DNA methylation) whose assay sites are spatially non-independent, and you need to quantify or visualize how correlation between adjacent CpG p-values decays with genomic distance before combining p-values. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-correlation-decay-visualization))
- **genomic-count-matrix-construction**: Use when raw ChIP-Seq BAM alignments plus an experiment design file must be converted into a tiled, normalized count matrix too large for memory, as preprocessing before GAM model fitting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-count-matrix-construction))
- **genomic-coverage-track-generation**: Use when paired-end MNase-seq or ATAC-seq BAM alignments must be converted into fragment-length-classified, midpoint-aggregated bedGraph count tracks to serve as HMM observables for chromatin occupancy profiling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-coverage-track-generation))
- **genomic-data-commons**: Query, search, and download cancer genomics data from the NCI Genomic Data Commons (GDC) REST API. Use when the user needs to find cases or files in TCGA, TARGET, CPTAC, FM, BEATAML, HCMI, MMRF, CGCI, or other GDC programs; pull clinical, biospecimen, copy-number, or somatic-mutation metadata; download open-access files or generate Data Transfer Tool manifests; perform BAM slicing by gene or region; retrieve gene expression matrices (FPKM-UQ), top-mutated-genes/SSMs analyses, survival data, or scRNA-Seq expression; manage saved cohorts; or run ad-hoc GraphQL queries against the GDC schema. ([jataware/beaker-biomedical](https://github.com/jataware/beaker-biomedical/tree/HEAD/src/beaker_biomedical/beaker_biomedical_context/skills/genomics-data-commons))
- **genomic-data-privacy-governance**: Use when a task involves planning privacy controls for human genomic or multi-omics data used in research and agent workflows to identify the research or operational question, target population or system, relevant version, sensitive data, and approval boundary before acting. Use current primary sources, produce a traceable artifact, and verify it against explicit criteria. Trigger for planning, analysis, review, or troubleshooting in this focused domain; do not execute external writes or clinical actions without authorization. ([Manoj-11-Dahal/try-Skills](https://github.com/Manoj-11-Dahal/try-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/science-research/life-sciences/genomic-data-privacy-governance) · ★3)
- **genomic-data-train-valid-test-splitting**: Use when raw task data downloaded for a DNALONGBENCH task must be partitioned and serialized into train*/valid*/test*.tfr files so downstream loaders and model training consume consistent splits. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-data-train-valid-test-splitting))
- **genomic-database-identifier-mapping**: Use when your gene expression matrix or pathway collection uses identifier formats incompatible with your enrichment analysis tool (e.g., gene symbols vs. Entrez IDs), or you need to reconcile gene sets from multiple sources (Reactome, KEGG) that employ different naming conventions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v1/skills/genomic-database-identifier-mapping))
- **genomic-distance-background-modeling**: Use when distance-normalized Hi-C or pcHi-C contact tables (covNorm normDistance output) need per-contact significance scores (p-value and FDR) so that reproducible significant long-range interactions can be detected. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-distance-background-modeling))
- **genomic-distance-binning-analysis**: Use when coverage-normalized Hi-C or pcHi-C fragment-pair tables (Rij residuals with cov_frag1, cov_frag2, freq, dist columns) need distance-dependent background removal before calling significant long-range interactions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-distance-binning-analysis))
- **genomic-distance-normalization-of-contact-probabilities**: Use when calling chromatin loop candidates from single-cell Hi-C contact matrices at 10-kb bin resolution and you need to control for the monotonic distance decay of contact probability before declaring a bin pair significant. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-distance-normalization-of-contact-probabilities))
- **genomic-distance-threshold-filtering**: Use when preparing Hi-C fragment files for haplotype assembly with SpecHap and you need to drop read pairs spanning more than 40 Mbp to avoid linkages with elevated trans interaction error rates. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-distance-threshold-filtering))
- **genomic-domain-boundary-refinement**: Use when calling broad ChIP-seq enrichment domains (e.g., H3K27me3/H3K9me3, kb-to-Mb) and you must convert multi-scale coarse-grained '1' blocks into precisely bounded candidate domains prior to Poisson/FDR significance assessment. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-domain-boundary-refinement))
- **genomic-enhancer-prediction**: Use when the goal is to classify genomic regions as enhancers from matched ChIP-seq (H3K4me1, H3K4me3, H3K27ac) and DNase-seq datasets, e.g. reproducing enhancer catalogs in human cell lines like HCT116 or HeLa. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-enhancer-prediction))
- **genomic-epidemiology**: Pathogen genomics for epidemiology — phylogenetics of outbreaks, transmission inference, and variant surveillance. ([aicodedecode/awesome-muse-skills](https://github.com/aicodedecode/awesome-muse-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/genomic-epidemiology) · ★68 · 3 copies)
- **genomic-epidemiology-reporting**: Use when in-silico virulence/typing gene calls from sequencing reads must be validated against PCR or another phenotypic reference across a clinical isolate cohort, with per-isolate concordance reported. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/genomic-epidemiology-reporting))
- **genomic-feature-annotation**: This skill is used to perform genomic feature annotation and visualization for any file containing genomic region information using Homer (Hypergeometric Optimization of Motif EnRichment). It annotates regions such as promoters, exons, introns, intergenic regions, and TSS proximity, and generates visual summaries of feature distributions. ChIPseeker mode is also supported according to requirements. ([diegosouzapw/awesome-omni-skill](https://github.com/diegosouzapw/awesome-omni-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/product/genomic-feature-annotation-majiayu000) · ★67 · 5 copies)
- **genomic-feature-annotation-overlap**: Use when after you have identified a set of differentially methylated bases or regions (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v1/skills/genomic-feature-annotation-overlap))
- **genomic-feature-collinearity-diagnostics**: Use when a multi-score genomic feature table (e.g., variant scores from multiple predictors plus epigenomic annotations) must be checked for pairwise redundancy/collinearity before feeding features into an integrative model such as ncDeepBrain. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-feature-collinearity-diagnostics))
- **genomic-feature-correlation-analysis**: Use when you have per-region expression estimates (e.g., uTAR pseudo-bulk coverage) plus a PCA loading matrix from single-cell expression data and need to ask whether the most highly expressed features are also the ones that define cell types. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/genomic-feature-correlation-analysis))
- **genomic-feature-engineering-from-chromatin-signals**: Use when genomic elements (candidate enhancers vs promoters) must be converted into numeric features from ChIP-seq/DNase-seq bedGraph signals to train or apply an enhancer-prediction classifier. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-feature-engineering-from-chromatin-signals))
- **genomic-feature-importance-ranking**: Use when you have labeled genomic interval sets (e.g. HOT vs COLD regions) with coordinates and reference genome sequences, and need to rank sequence or genomic features by predictive importance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-feature-importance-ranking))
- **genomic-feature-interval-derivation**: Use when you have a UCSC refGene.txt transcript annotation file plus chromInfo.txt for a genome assembly and need BED-like coordinate sets per feature class to feed feature-level analyses (e.g. scorePhastCons conservation scores). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-feature-interval-derivation))
- **genomic-feature-missingness-statistics**: Use when preparing variant-prioritization training matrices from ANNOVAR-annotated score columns (DANN, CADD, EIGEN, GWAVA, FATHMM) and you need to quantify per-score missingness before/after bPCA imputation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-feature-missingness-statistics))
- **genomic-feature-size-distribution-statistics**: Use when a catalog of genomic features (e.g. eccDNAs from WGS analysis plus literature curation) must be reduced to headline distribution statistics — total/provenance counts, mean/median feature size, per-feature segment counts, and copy numbers — for reproducibility checks. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-feature-size-distribution-statistics))
- **genomic-file-toolkit**: 当需要读写或解析 SAM/BAM/CRAM 比对、VCF/BCF 变异、FASTA/FASTQ 序列并提取区间、计算覆盖度时使用；用 pysam（htslib）做区域抓取、pileup 深度统计、变异过滤与序列提取，产出过滤后文件或统计结果；不适用于纯文本表格清洗、对齐/变异检测算法本身的实现、非基因组数据。触发词：pysam、bam、vcf、fastq、cram、bcf、fasta、samtools、bcftools、tabix、pileup、coverage、覆盖度、测序比对、变异、基因组区间、reference sequence、NGS、生物信息 ([findscripter/everything-skills](https://github.com/findscripter/everything-skills/tree/HEAD/09-verticals/genomic-file-toolkit) · ★11 · 2 copies)
- **genomic-flanking-sequence-extraction**: Use when variants (e.g., a VCF annotated by ANNOVAR ensGene) need underlying genomic sequence — exon plus flanks — retrieved from hg19 to build feature tables for splice-impact prediction. - **genomic-flanking-sequence-retrieval**: Use when you have coordinate-annotated modification sites (e.g., BED-format Ψ sites from RMBase) and need fixed-width sequence fragments centered on each site from a matching reference genome for downstream ML feature encoding. - **genomic-graph-representation-construction**: Use when input is a tab-separated N×N intra-chromosomal Hi-C contact matrix (e.g. dumped from .hic data via juicer_tools) and the goal is to represent genomic bins as graph nodes for GAE-based feature learning or clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/genomic-graph-representation-construction))
- **genomic-indel-feature-preparation**: Use when building supervised predictors for non-coding small indels (sindels) that need both generic functional annotations and tissue-specific epigenomic sequence-derived features, e.g. replicating the TIVAN-indel feature-set ablation. - **genomic-inquiry**: Default entry for natural-language DNA questions. The host agent resolves intent, reads focused skills, calls narrow evidence tools, and adapts after inspecting tool output.
